Contains the Renderer struct and all rendering-related functionality.\n4: \n5: pub mod frame;\n6: \n7: pub use frame::{Frame, TextPainter};\n8: \n9: use anyhow::Result;\n10: use fontdue::{Font, FontSettings, LineMetrics, Metrics};\n11: use softbuffer::Surface;\n12: use std::collections::HashMap;\n13: use std::num::NonZeroU32;\n14: use std::rc::Rc;\n15: use winit::window::Window;\n16: \n17: use token::model::editor_area::{EditorGroup, GroupId, Rect, SplitterBar, Tab};\n18: use token::model::{gutter_border_x, text_start_x, AppModel};\n19: \n20: pub type GlyphCacheKey = (char, u32);\n21: \n22: /// Get the display title for a tab.\n23: /// Centralizes the logic for determining what text to show in the tab bar.\n24: fn tab_title(model: &AppModel, tab: &Tab) -> String {\n25:     let editor = match model.editor_area.editors.get(&tab.editor_id) {\n26:         Some(e) => e,\n27:         None => return \"Untitled\".to_string(),\n28:     };\n29:     let doc_id = match editor.document_id {\n30:         Some(id) => id,\n31:         None => return \"Untitled\".to_string(),\n32:     };\n33:     let document = match model.editor_area.documents.get(&doc_id) {\n34:         Some(d) => d,\n35:         None => return \"Untitled\".to_string(),\n36:     };\n37:     document.display_name()\n38: }\n39: pub type GlyphCache = HashMap<GlyphCacheKey, (Metrics, Vec<u8>)>;\n40: \n41: pub const TAB_BAR_HEIGHT: usize = 28;\n42: pub const TABULATOR_WIDTH: usize = 4;\n43: \n44: pub fn expand_tabs_for_display(text: &str) -> String {\n45:     let mut result = String::with_capacity(text.len() * 2);\n46:     let mut visual_col = 0;\n47: \n48:     for ch in text.chars() {\n49:         if ch == '\\t' {\n50:             let spaces = TABULATOR_WIDTH - (visual_col % TABULATOR_WIDTH);\n51:             for _ in 0..spaces {\n52:                 result.push(' ');\n53:             }\n54:             visual_col += spaces;\n55:         } else {\n56:             result.push(ch);\n57:             visual_col += 1;\n58:         }\n59:     }\n60: \n61:     result\n62: }\n63: \n64: pub fn char_col_to_visual_col(text: &str, char_col: usize) -> usize {\n65:     let mut visual_col = 0;\n66:     for (i, ch) in text.chars().enumerate() {\n67:         if i >= char_col {\n68:             break;\n69:         }\n70:         if ch == '\\t' {\n71:             visual_col += TABULATOR_WIDTH - (visual_col % TABULATOR_WIDTH);\n72:         } else {\n73:             visual_col += 1;\n74:         }\n75:     }\n76:     visual_col\n77: }\n78: \n79: pub fn visual_col_to_char_col(text: &str, visual_col: usize) -> usize {\n80:     let mut current_visual = 0;\n81:     let mut char_col = 0;\n82: \n83:     for ch in text.chars() {\n84:         if current_visual >= visual_col {\n85:             return char_col;\n86:         }\n87: \n88:         if ch == '\\t' {\n89:             let tab_width = TABULATOR_WIDTH - (current_visual % TABULATOR_WIDTH);\n90:             current_visual += tab_width;\n91:         } else {\n92:             current_visual += 1;\n93:         }\n94:         char_col += 1;\n95:     }\n96: \n97:     char_col\n98: }\n99: \n100: pub struct Renderer {\n101:     font: Font,\n102:     surface: Surface<Rc<Window>, Rc<Window>>,\n103:     width: u32,\n104:     height: u32,\n105:     font_size: f32,\n106:     line_metrics: LineMetrics,\n107:     glyph_cache: GlyphCache,\n108:     char_width: f32,\n109: }\n110: \n111: impl Renderer {\n112:     pub fn new(window: Rc<Window>, context: &softbuffer::Context<Rc<Window>>) -> Result<Self> {\n113:         let scale_factor = window.scale_factor();\n114:         let (width, height) = {\n115:             let size = window.inner_size();\n116:             (size.width, size.height)\n117:         };\n118: \n119:         let surface = Surface::new(context, Rc::clone(&window))\n120:             .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"Failed to create surface: {}\", e))?;\n121: \n122:         let font = Font::from_bytes(\n123:             include_bytes!(\"../../assets/JetBrainsMono.ttf\") as &[u8],\n124:             FontSettings::default(),\n125:         )\n126:         .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"Failed to load font: {}\", e))?;\n127: \n128:         let font_size = 14.0 * scale_factor as f32;\n129: \n130:         let line_metrics = font\n131:             .horizontal_line_metrics(font_size)\n132:             .expect(\"Font missing horizontal line metrics\");\n133: \n134:         let (metrics, _) = font.rasterize('M', font_size);\n135:         let char_width = metrics.advance_width;\n136: \n137:         Ok(Self {\n138:             font,\n139:             surface,\n140:             width,\n141:             height,\n142:             font_size,\n143:             line_metrics,\n144:             glyph_cache: HashMap::new(),\n145:             char_width,\n146:         })\n147:     }\n148: \n149:     pub fn char_width(&self) -> f32 {\n150:         self.char_width\n151:     }\n152: \n153:     #[allow(dead_code)]\n154:     pub fn font(&self) -> &Font {\n155:         &self.font\n156:     }\n157: \n158:     #[allow(dead_code)]\n159:     pub fn font_size(&self) -> f32 {\n160:         self.font_size\n161:     }\n162: \n163:     #[allow(dead_code)]\n164:     pub fn line_height(&self) -> usize {\n165:         self.line_metrics.new_line_size.ceil() as usize\n166:     }\n167: \n168:     #[allow(dead_code)]\n169:     pub fn ascent(&self) -> f32 {\n170:         self.line_metrics.ascent\n171:     }\n172: \n173:     #[allow(dead_code)]\n174:     pub fn line_metrics(&self) -> &LineMetrics {\n175:         &self.line_metrics\n176:     }\n177: \n178:     #[allow(dead_code)]\n179:     pub fn glyph_cache_mut(&mut self) -> &mut GlyphCache {\n180:         &mut self.glyph_cache\n181:     }\n182: \n183:     #[allow(dead_code)]\n184:     pub fn dimensions(&self) -> (u32, u32) {\n185:         (self.width, self.height)\n186:     }\n187: \n188:     fn render_all_groups_static(\n189:         frame: &mut Frame,\n190:         painter: &mut TextPainter,\n191:         model: &AppModel,\n192:         splitters: &[SplitterBar],\n193:         line_height: usize,\n194:         char_width: f32,\n195:     ) {\n196:         for (&group_id, group) in &model.editor_area.groups {\n197:             let is_focused = group_id == model.editor_area.focused_group_id;\n198:             Self::render_editor_group_static(\n199:                 frame,\n200:                 painter,\n201:                 model,\n202:                 group_id,\n203:                 group.rect,\n204:                 is_focused,\n205:                 line_height,\n206:                 char_width,\n207:             );\n208:         }\n209: \n210:         Self::render_splitters_static(frame, splitters, model);\n211:     }\n212: \n213:     fn render_editor_group_static(\n214:         frame: &mut Frame,\n215:         painter: &mut TextPainter,\n216:         model: &AppModel,\n217:         group_id: GroupId,\n218:         group_rect: Rect,\n219:         is_focused: bool,\n220:         line_height: usize,\n221:         char_width: f32,\n222:     ) {\n223:         let group = match model.editor_area.groups.get(&group_id) {\n224:             Some(g) => g,\n225:             None => return,\n226:         };\n227: \n228:         let editor_id = match group.active_editor_id() {\n229:             Some(id) => id,\n230:             None => return,\n231:         };\n232: \n233:         let editor = match model.editor_area.editors.get(&editor_id) {\n234:             Some(e) => e,\n235:             None => return,\n236:         };\n237: \n238:         let doc_id = match editor.document_id {\n239:             Some(id) => id,\n240:             None => return,\n241:         };\n242: \n243:         let document = match model.editor_area.documents.get(&doc_id) {\n244:             Some(d) => d,\n245:             None => return,\n246:         };\n247: \n248:         let rect_x = group_rect.x as usize;\n249:         let rect_y = group_rect.y as usize;\n250:         let rect_w = group_rect.width as usize;\n251:         let rect_h = group_rect.height as usize;\n252: \n253:         Self::render_tab_bar_static(\n254:             frame, painter, model, group, rect_x, rect_y, rect_w, char_width,\n255:         );\n256: \n257:         let content_y = rect_y + TAB_BAR_HEIGHT;\n258:         let content_h = rect_h.saturating_sub(TAB_BAR_HEIGHT);\n259: \n260:         let text_start_x_offset = text_start_x(char_width).round() as usize;\n261:         let group_text_start_x = rect_x + text_start_x_offset;\n262: \n263:         let visible_lines = content_h / line_height;\n264:         let end_line = (editor.viewport.top_line + visible_lines).min(document.buffer.len_lines());\n265: \n266:         // Highlight primary cursor line only\n267:         let current_line_color = model.theme.editor.current_line_background.to_argb_u32();\n268:         if editor.active_cursor().line >= editor.viewport.top_line\n269:             && editor.active_cursor().line < end_line\n270:         {\n271:             let screen_line = editor.active_cursor().line - editor.viewport.top_line;\n272:             let highlight_y = content_y + screen_line * line_height;\n273:             let highlight_h = line_height.min(content_y + content_h - highlight_y);\n274:             frame.fill_rect_px(rect_x, highlight_y, rect_w, highlight_h, current_line_color);\n275:         }\n276: \n277:         // Render ALL selections (primary + secondary cursors)\n278:         let selection_color = model.theme.editor.selection_background.to_argb_u32();\n279:         for selection in &editor.selections {\n280:             if selection.is_empty() {\n281:                 continue;\n282:             }\n283: \n284:             let sel_start = selection.start();\n285:             let sel_end = selection.end();\n286: \n287:             for doc_line in editor.viewport.top_line..end_line {\n288:                 if doc_line < sel_start.line || doc_line > sel_end.line {\n289:                     continue;\n290:                 }\n291: \n292:                 let screen_line = doc_line - editor.viewport.top_line;\n293:                 let y_start = content_y + screen_line * line_height;\n294:                 let y_end = (y_start + line_height).min(content_y + content_h);\n295: \n296:                 let line_len = document.line_length(doc_line);\n297: \n298:                 let line_text = document.get_line(doc_line).unwrap_or_default();\n299:                 let line_text_trimmed = if line_text.ends_with('\\n') {\n300:                     &line_text[..line_text.len() - 1]\n301:                 } else {\n302:                     &line_text\n303:                 };\n304: \n305:                 let start_col = if doc_line == sel_start.line {\n306:                     sel_start.column\n307:                 } else {\n308:                     0\n309:                 };\n310:                 let end_col = if doc_line == sel_end.line {\n311:                     sel_end.column\n312:                 } else {\n313:                     line_len\n314:                 };\n315: \n316:                 let visual_start_col = char_col_to_visual_col(line_text_trimmed, start_col);\n317:                 let visual_end_col = char_col_to_visual_col(line_text_trimmed, end_col);\n318: \n319:                 let visible_start_col =\n320:                     visual_start_col.saturating_sub(editor.viewport.left_column);\n321:                 let visible_end_col = visual_end_col.saturating_sub(editor.viewport.left_column);\n322: \n323:                 let x_start =\n324:                     group_text_start_x + (visible_start_col as f32 * char_width).round() as usize;\n325:                 let x_end = (group_text_start_x\n326:                     + (visible_end_col as f32 * char_width).round() as usize)\n327:                     .min(rect_x + rect_w);\n328: \n329:                 frame.fill_rect_px(\n330:                     x_start,\n331:                     y_start,\n332:                     x_end.saturating_sub(x_start),\n333:                     y_end.saturating_sub(y_start),\n334:                     selection_color,\n335:                 );\n336:             }\n337:         }\n338: \n339:         let line_num_color = model.theme.gutter.foreground.to_argb_u32();\n340:         let line_num_active_color = model.theme.gutter.foreground_active.to_argb_u32();\n341:         let text_color = model.theme.editor.foreground.to_argb_u32();\n342: \n343:         for (screen_line, doc_line) in (editor.viewport.top_line..end_line).enumerate() {\n344:             if let Some(line_text) = document.get_line(doc_line) {\n345:                 let y = content_y + screen_line * line_height;\n346:                 if y >= content_y + content_h {\n347:                     break;\n348:                 }\n349: \n350:                 let line_num_str = format!(\"{:4} \", doc_line + 1);\n351:                 let line_color = if doc_line == editor.active_cursor().line {\n352:                     line_num_active_color\n353:                 } else {\n354:                     line_num_color\n355:                 };\n356:                 painter.draw(frame, rect_x, y, &line_num_str, line_color);\n357: \n358:                 let visible_text = if line_text.ends_with('\\n') {\n359:                     &line_text[..line_text.len() - 1]\n360:                 } else {\n361:                     &line_text\n362:                 };\n363: \n364:                 let expanded_text = expand_tabs_for_display(visible_text);\n365: \n366:                 let max_chars =\n367:                     ((rect_w as f32 - text_start_x_offset as f32) / char_width).floor() as usize;\n368:                 let display_text: String = expanded_text\n369:                     .chars()\n370:                     .skip(editor.viewport.left_column)\n371:                     .take(max_chars)\n372:                     .collect();\n373: \n374:                 painter.draw(frame, group_text_start_x, y, &display_text, text_color);\n375:             }\n376:         }\n377: \n378:         // Draw cursors\n379:         if model.ui.cursor_visible {\n380:             let actual_visible_columns =\n381:                 ((rect_w as f32 - text_start_x_offset as f32) / char_width).floor() as usize;\n382:             let primary_cursor_color = model.theme.editor.cursor_color.to_argb_u32();\n383:             let secondary_cursor_color = model.theme.editor.secondary_cursor_color.to_argb_u32();\n384: \n385:             for (idx, cursor) in editor.cursors.iter().enumerate() {\n386:                 let cursor_in_vertical_view = cursor.line >= editor.viewport.top_line\n387:                     && cursor.line < editor.viewport.top_line + visible_lines;\n388: \n389:                 let line_text = document.get_line(cursor.line).unwrap_or_default();\n390:                 let line_text_trimmed = if line_text.ends_with('\\n') {\n391:                     &line_text[..line_text.len() - 1]\n392:                 } else {\n393:                     &line_text\n394:                 };\n395:                 let visual_cursor_col = char_col_to_visual_col(line_text_trimmed, cursor.column);\n396: \n397:                 let cursor_in_horizontal_view = visual_cursor_col >= editor.viewport.left_column\n398:                     && visual_cursor_col < editor.viewport.left_column + actual_visible_columns;\n399: \n400:                 if cursor_in_vertical_view && cursor_in_horizontal_view {\n401:                     let screen_line = cursor.line - editor.viewport.top_line;\n402:                     let cursor_visual_column = visual_cursor_col - editor.viewport.left_column;\n403:                     let x = (group_text_start_x as f32 + cursor_visual_column as f32 * char_width)\n404:                         .round() as usize;\n405:                     let y = content_y + screen_line * line_height;\n406: \n407:                     // TODO: check if this will \"always\" be the primary cursor, we also have \"active_cursor\", which is unclear the difference  between them\n408:                     let cursor_color = if idx == 0 {\n409:                         primary_cursor_color\n410:                     } else {\n411:                         secondary_cursor_color\n412:                     };\n413: \n414:                     // Cursor: 2px wide, line_height - 2 tall, offset by 1px from top\n415:                     frame.fill_rect_px(x, y + 1, 2, line_height.saturating_sub(2), cursor_color);\n416:                 }\n417:             }\n418:         }\n419: \n420:         // Gutter border\n421:         let gutter_border_color = model.theme.gutter.border_color.to_argb_u32();\n422:         let border_x = rect_x + gutter_border_x(char_width).round() as usize;\n423:         frame.fill_rect_px(border_x, content_y, 1, content_h, gutter_border_color);\n424: \n425:         // Dim non-focused groups when multiple groups exist (4% black overlay)\n426:         if !is_focused && model.editor_area.groups.len() > 1 {\n427:             let dim_color = 0x0A000000_u32; // 4% opacity black (alpha = 10/255 ≈ 4%)\n428:             frame.blend_rect(group_rect, dim_color);\n429:         }\n430:     }\n431: \n432:     fn render_tab_bar_static(\n433:         frame: &mut Frame,\n434:         painter: &mut TextPainter,\n435:         model: &AppModel,\n436:         group: &EditorGroup,\n437:         rect_x: usize,\n438:         rect_y: usize,\n439:         rect_w: usize,\n440:         char_width: f32,\n441:     ) {\n442:         let tab_bar_bg = model.theme.tab_bar.background.to_argb_u32();\n443:         frame.fill_rect_px(rect_x, rect_y, rect_w, TAB_BAR_HEIGHT, tab_bar_bg);\n444: \n445:         let border_color = model.theme.tab_bar.border.to_argb_u32();\n446:         let border_y = (rect_y + TAB_BAR_HEIGHT).saturating_sub(1);\n447:         frame.fill_rect_px(rect_x, border_y, rect_w, 1, border_color);\n448: \n449:         let mut tab_x = rect_x + 4;\n450:         let tab_height = TAB_BAR_HEIGHT - 4;\n451:         let tab_y = rect_y + 2;\n452: \n453:         for (idx, tab) in group.tabs.iter().enumerate() {\n454:             let is_active = idx == group.active_tab_index;\n455:             let display_name = tab_title(model, tab);\n456:             let tab_width = (display_name.len() as f32 * char_width).round() as usize + 16;\n457: \n458:             let (bg_color, fg_color) = if is_active {\n459:                 (\n460:                     model.theme.tab_bar.active_background.to_argb_u32(),\n461:                     model.theme.tab_bar.active_foreground.to_argb_u32(),\n462:                 )\n463:             } else {\n464:                 (\n465:                     model.theme.tab_bar.inactive_background.to_argb_u32(),\n466:                     model.theme.tab_bar.inactive_foreground.to_argb_u32(),\n467:                 )\n468:             };\n469: \n470:             let actual_tab_width = tab_width.min(rect_x + rect_w - tab_x);\n471:             frame.fill_rect_px(tab_x, tab_y, actual_tab_width, tab_height, bg_color);\n472: \n473:             let text_x = tab_x + 8;\n474:             let text_y = tab_y + 4;\n475:             painter.draw(frame, text_x, text_y, &display_name, fg_color);\n476: \n477:             tab_x += tab_width + 2;\n478:             if tab_x >= rect_x + rect_w {\n479:                 break;\n480:             }\n481:         }\n482:     }\n483: \n484:     fn render_splitters_static(frame: &mut Frame, splitters: &[SplitterBar], model: &AppModel) {\n485:         let splitter_color = model.theme.splitter.background.to_argb_u32();\n486: \n487:         for splitter in splitters {\n488:             frame.fill_rect(splitter.rect, splitter_color);\n489:         }\n490:     }\n491: \n492:     pub fn render(\n493:         &mut self,\n494:         model: &mut AppModel,\n495:         perf: &mut crate::runtime::perf::PerfStats,\n496:     ) -> Result<()> {\n497:         perf.reset_frame_stats();\n498: \n499:         if self.width != model.window_size.0 || self.height != model.window_size.1 {\n500:             self.width = model.window_size.0;\n501:             self.height = model.window_size.1;\n502:             self.surface\n503:                 .resize(\n504:                     NonZeroU32::new(self.width).unwrap(),\n505:                     NonZeroU32::new(self.height).unwrap(),\n506:                 )\n507:                 .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"Failed to resize surface: {}\", e))?;\n508:         }\n509: \n510:         let line_height = self.line_metrics.new_line_size.ceil() as usize;\n511:         let font_size = self.font_size;\n512:         let ascent = self.line_metrics.ascent;\n513:         let char_width = self.char_width;\n514:         let width = self.width;\n515:         let height = self.height;\n516: \n517:         let status_bar_height = line_height;\n518:         let available_rect = Rect::new(\n519:             0.0,\n520:             0.0,\n521:             width as f32,\n522:             (height as usize).saturating_sub(status_bar_height) as f32,\n523:         );\n524:         let splitters = model.editor_area.compute_layout(available_rect);\n525: \n526:         let mut buffer = self\n527:             .surface\n528:             .buffer_mut()\n529:             .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"Failed to get surface buffer: {}\", e))?;\n530: \n531:         // Create Frame wrapper for cleaner drawing API\n532:         // Note: We use Frame for new code; legacy code still uses raw buffer slices\n533:         let width_usize = width as usize;\n534:         let height_usize = height as usize;\n535: \n536:         {\n537:             let _timer = perf.time_clear();\n538:             let bg_color = model.theme.editor.background.to_argb_u32();\n539:             let mut frame = Frame::new(&mut buffer, width_usize, height_usize);\n540:             frame.clear(bg_color);\n541:         }\n542: \n543:         {\n544:             let _timer = perf.time_text();\n545:             let mut frame = Frame::new(&mut buffer, width_usize, height_usize);\n546:             let mut painter =\n547:                 TextPainter::new(&self.font, &mut self.glyph_cache, font_size, ascent);\n548:             Self::render_all_groups_static(\n549:                 &mut frame,\n550:                 &mut painter,\n551:                 model,\n552:                 &splitters,\n553:                 line_height,\n554:                 char_width,\n555:             );\n556:         }\n557: \n558:         {\n559:             let _timer = perf.time_status_bar();\n560:             let status_bar_bg = model.theme.status_bar.background.to_argb_u32();\n561:             let status_bar_fg = model.theme.status_bar.foreground.to_argb_u32();\n562:             let status_y = (height as usize).saturating_sub(status_bar_height);\n563: \n564:             // Use Frame for status bar background\n565:             {\n566:                 let mut frame = Frame::new(&mut buffer, width_usize, height_usize);\n567:                 frame.fill_rect_px(0, status_y, width_usize, status_bar_height, status_bar_bg);\n568:             }\n569: \n570:             let available_chars = (width as f32 / char_width).floor() as usize;\n571:             let layout = model.ui.status_bar.layout(available_chars);\n572: \n573:             // Use TextPainter for status bar text\n574:             {\n575:                 let mut frame = Frame::new(&mut buffer, width_usize, height_usize);\n576:                 let mut painter =\n577:                     TextPainter::new(&self.font, &mut self.glyph_cache, font_size, ascent);\n578: \n579:                 for seg in &layout.left {\n580:                     let x_px = (seg.x as f32 * char_width).round() as usize;\n581:                     painter.draw(&mut frame, x_px, status_y + 2, &seg.text, status_bar_fg);\n582:                 }\n583: \n584:                 for seg in &layout.right {\n585:                     let x_px = (seg.x as f32 * char_width).round() as usize;\n586:                     painter.draw(&mut frame, x_px, status_y + 2, &seg.text, status_bar_fg);\n587:                 }\n588: \n589:                 // Draw separators\n590:                 let separator_color = model\n591:                     .theme\n592:                     .status_bar\n593:                     .foreground\n594:                     .with_alpha(100)\n595:                     .to_argb_u32();\n596:                 for &sep_char_x in &layout.separator_positions {\n597:                     let x_px = (sep_char_x as f32 * char_width).round() as usize;\n598:                     frame.fill_rect_px(x_px, status_y, 1, status_bar_height, separator_color);\n599:                 }\n600:             }\n601:         }\n602: Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::Paste))\n100:         }\n101: \n102:         // Duplicate line/selection (Cmd+D on macOS, Ctrl+D elsewhere)\n103:         Key::Character(ref s) if s.eq_ignore_ascii_case(\"d\") && (ctrl || logo) => {\n104:             update(model, Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::Duplicate))\n105:         }\n106: \n107:         // Select next occurrence (Cmd+J on macOS, Ctrl+J elsewhere)\n108:         Key::Character(ref s) if s.eq_ignore_ascii_case(\"j\") && (ctrl || logo) && !shift => {\n109:             update(model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::SelectNextOccurrence))\n110:         }\n111: \n112:         // Unselect last occurrence (Shift+Cmd+J on macOS, Shift+Ctrl+J elsewhere)\n113:         Key::Character(ref s) if s.eq_ignore_ascii_case(\"j\") && (ctrl || logo) && shift => {\n114:             update(model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::UnselectOccurrence))\n115:         }\n116: \n117:         // === Split View Shortcuts ===\n118: \n119:         // Split horizontal (Shift+Option+Cmd+H)\n120:         Key::Character(ref s) if s.eq_ignore_ascii_case(\"h\") && logo && shift && alt => update(\n121:             model,\n122:             Msg::Layout(LayoutMsg::SplitFocused(SplitDirection::Horizontal)),\n123:         ),\n124: \n125:         // Split vertical (Shift+Option+Cmd+V)\n126:         Key::Character(ref s) if s.eq_ignore_ascii_case(\"v\") && logo && shift && alt => update(\n127:             model,\n128:             Msg::Layout(LayoutMsg::SplitFocused(SplitDirection::Vertical)),\n129:         ),\n130: \n131:         // New tab (Shift+Cmd+N)\n132:         Key::Character(ref s) if s.eq_ignore_ascii_case(\"n\") && logo && shift && !alt => {\n133:             update(model, Msg::Layout(LayoutMsg::NewTab))\n134:         }\n135: \n136:         // Close tab (Cmd+W)\n137:         Key::Character(ref s) if s.eq_ignore_ascii_case(\"w\") && logo && !shift && !alt => {\n138:             update(model, Msg::Layout(LayoutMsg::CloseFocusedTab))\n139:         }\n140: \n141:         // Next tab (Option+Cmd+Right)\n142:         Key::Named(NamedKey::ArrowRight) if logo && alt && !shift => {\n143:             update(model, Msg::Layout(LayoutMsg::NextTab))\n144:         }\n145: \n146:         // Previous tab (Option+Cmd+Left)\n147:         Key::Named(NamedKey::ArrowLeft) if logo && alt && !shift => {\n148:             update(model, Msg::Layout(LayoutMsg::PrevTab))\n149:         }\n150: \n[... omitted lines 151 to ∞ ...]",

This module contains all the state types following the Elm Architecture pattern.\n4: \n5: pub mod document;\n6: pub mod editor;\n7: pub mod editor_area;\n8: pub mod status_bar;\n9: pub mod ui;\n10: \n11: pub use document::{Document, EditOperation};\n12: pub use editor::{\n13:     Cursor, EditorState, OccurrenceState, Position, RectangleSelectionState, ScrollRevealMode,\n14:     Selection, Viewport,\n15: };\n16: pub use editor_area::{\n17:     DocumentId, EditorArea, EditorGroup, EditorId, GroupId, LayoutNode, Rect, SplitContainer,\n18:     SplitDirection, SplitterBar, Tab, TabId, SPLITTER_WIDTH,\n19: };\n20: pub use status_bar::{\n21:     sync_status_bar, RenderedSegment, SegmentContent, SegmentId, SegmentPosition, StatusBar,\n22:     StatusBarLayout, StatusSegment, TransientMessage,\n23: };\n24: pub use ui::UiState;\n25: \n26: #[cfg(debug_assertions)]\n27: use crate::debug_overlay::DebugOverlay;\n28: use crate::theme::Theme;\n29: use std::path::PathBuf;\n30: \n31: /// Layout constant - width of line number gutter in characters (e.g., \" 123 \")\n32: pub const LINE_NUMBER_GUTTER_CHARS: usize = 5;\n33: /// Padding after line numbers, before the gutter border (pixels)\n34: pub const GUTTER_PADDING_PX: f32 = 4.0;\n35: /// Padding after the gutter border, before text content (pixels)  \n36: pub const TEXT_AREA_PADDING_PX: f32 = 8.0;\n37: \n38: /// Calculate the x-coordinate where text content begins\n39: #[inline]\n40: pub fn text_start_x(char_width: f32) -> f32 {\n41:     let gutter_width = char_width * LINE_NUMBER_GUTTER_CHARS as f32 + GUTTER_PADDING_PX;\n42:     let border_width = 1.0;\n43:     gutter_width + border_width + TEXT_AREA_PADDING_PX\n44: }\n45: \n46: /// Calculate the x-coordinate of the gutter border\n47: #[inline]\n48: pub fn gutter_border_x(char_width: f32) -> f32 {\n49:     char_width * LINE_NUMBER_GUTTER_CHARS as f32 + GUTTER_PADDING_PX\n50: }\n51: \n52: /// The complete application model\n53: #[derive(Debug)]\n54: pub struct AppModel {\n55:     /// Editor area containing all documents, editors, groups, and layout\n56:     pub editor_area: EditorArea,\n57:     /// UI state (status bar, cursor blink)\n58:     pub ui: UiState,\n59:     /// Theme for colors and styling\n60:     pub theme: Theme,\n61:     /// Window dimensions\n62:     pub window_size: (u32, u32),\n63:     /// Line height in pixels\n64:     pub line_height: usize,\n65:     /// Character width in pixels (monospace)\n66:     pub char_width: f32,\n67:     /// Debug overlay state (debug builds only)\n68:     #[cfg(debug_assertions)]\n69:     pub debug_overlay: Option<DebugOverlay>,\n70: }\n71: \n72: impl AppModel {\n73:     /// Create a new application model with the given window size\n74:     pub fn new(window_width: u32, window_height: u32, file_paths: Vec<PathBuf>) -> Self {\n75:         let line_height = 20;\n76:         let char_width: f32 = 10.0; // Will be corrected by renderer with actual font metrics\n77: \n78:         // Calculate viewport dimensions\n79:         let text_x = text_start_x(char_width).round();\n80:         let visible_columns = ((window_width as f32 - text_x) / char_width).floor() as usize;\n81:         let status_bar_height = line_height;\n82:         let visible_lines =\n83:             (window_height as usize).saturating_sub(status_bar_height) / line_height;\n84: \n85:         // Load first file or create demo document\n86:         let (first_document, status_message) = if let Some(first_path) = file_paths.first() {\n87:             match Document::from_file(first_path.clone()) {\n88:                 Ok(doc) => {\n89:                     let msg = if file_paths.len() > 1 {\n90:                         format!(\"Opened {} files\", file_paths.len())\n91:                     } else {\n92:                         format!(\"Loaded: {}\", first_path.display())\n93:                     };\n94:                     (doc, msg)\n95:                 }\n96:                 Err(e) => {\n97:                     let msg = format!(\"Error loading {}: {}\", first_path.display(), e);\n98:                     (Document::new(), msg)\n99:                 }\n100:             }\n101:         } else {\n102:             let doc = Document::with_text(\n103:                 \"Hello, World!\\nThis is a text editor built in Rust.\\nUsing Elm architecture!\\n\\nStart typing to edit.\\n\"\n104:             );\n105:             (doc, \"New file\".to_string())\n106:         };\n107: \n108:         // Create editor state with viewport\n109:         let editor = EditorState::with_viewport(visible_lines, visible_columns);\n110: \n111:         // Create editor area with first document\n112:         let mut editor_area = EditorArea::single_document(first_document, editor);\n113: \n114:         // Open additional files as tabs\n115:         for path in file_paths.into_iter().skip(1) {\n116:             let doc_id = editor_area.next_document_id();\n117:             match Document::from_file(path.clone()) {\n118:                 Ok(mut doc) => {\n119:                     doc.id = Some(doc_id);\n120:                     editor_area.documents.insert(doc_id, doc);\n121: \n122:                     // Create editor for this document\n123:                     let editor_id = editor_area.next_editor_id();\n124:                     let mut editor = EditorState::with_viewport(visible_lines, visible_columns);\n125:                     editor.id = Some(editor_id);\n126:                     editor.document_id = Some(doc_id);\n127:                     editor_area.editors.insert(editor_id, editor);\n128: \n129:                     // Create tab in focused group\n130:                     let tab_id = editor_area.next_tab_id();\n131:                     let tab = Tab {\n132:                         id: tab_id,\n133:                         editor_id,\n134:                         is_pinned: false,\n135:                         is_preview: false,\n136:                     };\n137: \n138:                     if let Some(group) = editor_area.groups.get_mut(&editor_area.focused_group_id) {\n139:                         group.tabs.push(tab);\n140:                     }\n141:                 }\n142:                 Err(e) => {\n143:                     tracing::warn!(\"Failed to open {}: {}\", path.display(), e);\n144:                 }\n145:             }\n146:         }\n147: \n148:         Self {\n149:             editor_area,\n150:             ui: UiState::with_status(status_message),\n151:             theme: Theme::default(),\n152:             window_size: (window_width, window_height),\n153:             line_height,\n154:             char_width,\n155:             #[cfg(debug_assertions)]\n156:             debug_overlay: Some(DebugOverlay::new()),\n157:         }\n158:     }\n159: \n160:     /// Get the focused editor (read-only), or None if no editor is focused\n161:     /// Used for debug instrumentation\n162:     pub fn focused_editor(&self) -> Option<&EditorState> {\n163:         self.editor_area.focused_editor()\n164:     }\n165: \n166:     // =========================================================================\n167:     // Accessor methods for backward compatibility\n168:     // These delegate to editor_area's focused document/editor\n169:     // =========================================================================\n170: \n171:     /// Get the focused document (read-only)\n172:     #[inline]\n173:     pub fn document(&self) -> &Document {\n174:         self.editor_area\n175:             .focused_document()\n176:             .expect(\"EditorArea must have at least one document\")\n177:     }\n178: \n179:     /// Get the focused document (mutable)\n180:     #[inline]\n181:     pub fn document_mut(&mut self) -> &mut Document {\n182:         self.editor_area\n183:             .focused_document_mut()\n184:             .expect(\"EditorArea must have at least one document\")\n185:     }\n186: \n187:     /// Get the focused editor state (read-only)\n188:     #[inline]\n189:     pub fn editor(&self) -> &EditorState {\n190:         self.editor_area\n191:             .focused_editor()\n192:             .expect(\"EditorArea must have at least one editor\")\n193:     }\n194: \n195:     /// Get the focused editor state (mutable)\n196:     #[inline]\n197:     pub fn editor_mut(&mut self) -> &mut EditorState {\n198:         self.editor_area\n199:             .focused_editor_mut()\n200:             .expect(\"EditorArea must have at least one editor\")\n201:     }\n202: \n203:     /// Update viewport dimensions after window resize\n204:     /// Updates ALL editors, not just the focused one (for split view support)\n205:     pub fn resize(&mut self, width: u32, height: u32) {\n206:         self.window_size = (width, height);\n207: \n208:         let text_x = text_start_x(self.char_width).round();\n209:         let visible_columns = ((width as f32 - text_x) / self.char_width).floor() as usize;\n210:         // Subtract status bar height (1 line) from available height\n211:         let status_bar_height = self.line_height;\n212:         let visible_lines = (height as usize).saturating_sub(status_bar_height) / self.line_height;\n213: \n214:         // FIX: Update ALL editors, not just the focused one\n215:         for editor in self.editor_area.editors.values_mut() {\n216:             editor.resize_viewport(visible_lines, visible_columns);\n217:         }\n218:     }\n219: \n220:     /// Update char_width from actual font metrics\n221:     /// Updates ALL editors for split view support\n222:     pub fn set_char_width(&mut self, char_width: f32) {\n223:         self.char_width = char_width;\n224: \n225:         // Recalculate visible columns with new char width\n226:         let text_x = text_start_x(char_width).round();\n227:         let visible_columns = ((self.window_size.0 as f32 - text_x) / char_width).floor() as usize;\n228: \n229:         // FIX: Update ALL editors, not just the focused one\n230:         for editor in self.editor_area.editors.values_mut() {\n231:             editor.viewport.visible_columns = visible_columns;\n232:         }\n233:     }\n234: \n235:     // Convenience methods that delegate to sub-models\n236: \n237:     /// Get the buffer offset for the current cursor position\n238:     pub fn cursor_buffer_position(&self) -> usize {\n239:         self.editor().cursor_offset(self.document())\n240:     }\n241: \n242:     /// Set cursor position from buffer offset (clears selection)\n243:     pub fn set_cursor_from_position(&mut self, pos: usize) {\n244:         let doc = self\n245:             .editor_area\n246:             .focused_document()\n247:             .expect(\"must have document\");\n248:         let (line, column) = doc.offset_to_cursor(pos);\n249:         let editor = self.editor_mut();\n250:         editor.cursors[0].line = line;\n251:         editor.cursors[0].column = column;\n252:         editor.cursors[0].desired_column = None;\n253:         editor.clear_selection();\n254:     }\n255: \n256:     /// Move cursor to buffer offset without clearing selection\n257:     pub fn move_cursor_to_position(&mut self, pos: usize) {\n258:         let doc = self\n259:             .editor_area\n260:             .focused_document()\n261:             .expect(\"must have document\");\n262:         let (line, column) = doc.offset_to_cursor(pos);\n263:         let editor = self.editor_mut();\n264:         editor.cursors[0].line = line;\n265:         editor.cursors[0].column = column;\n266:         editor.cursors[0].desired_column = None;\n267:     }\n268: \n269:     /// Get the current line length\n270:     pub fn current_line_length(&self) -> usize {\n271:         self.editor().current_line_length(self.document())\n272:     }\n273: \n274:     /// Ensure cursor is visible in viewport (minimal scroll)\n275:     /// Uses EditorArea helper to avoid cloning the document\n276:     pub fn ensure_cursor_visible(&mut self) {\n277:         self.editor_area\n278:             .ensure_focused_cursor_visible(ScrollRevealMode::Minimal);\n279:     }\n280: \n281:     /// Ensure cursor is visible with direction-aware alignment\n282:     ///\n283:     /// When moving up, cursor is revealed at the top of the safe zone.\n284:     /// When moving down, cursor is revealed at the bottom of the safe zone.\n285:     /// For horizontal movement or no hint, uses minimal scroll.\n286:     /// Uses EditorArea helper to avoid cloning the document\n287:     pub fn ensure_cursor_visible_directional(&mut self, vertical_up: Option<bool>) {\n288:         let mode = match vertical_up {\n289:             Some(true) => ScrollRevealMode::TopAligned,\n290:             Some(false) => ScrollRevealMode::BottomAligned,\n291:             None => ScrollRevealMode::Minimal,\n292:         };\n293:         self.editor_area.ensure_focused_cursor_visible(mode);\n294:     }\n295: \n296:     /// Reset cursor blink timer\n297:     pub fn reset_cursor_blink(&mut self) {\n298:         self.ui.reset_cursor_blink();\n299:     }\n300: \n301:     /// Get a line from the document\n302:     pub fn get_line(&self, line_idx: usize) -> Option<String> {\n303:         self.document().get_line(line_idx)\n304:     }\n305: \n306:     /// Get line length\n307:     pub fn line_length(&self, line_idx: usize) -> usize {\n308:         self.document().line_length(line_idx)\n309:     }\n310: \n311:     /// Get first non-whitespace column on current line\n312:     pub fn first_non_whitespace_column(&self) -> usize {\n313:         self.document()\n314:             .first_non_whitespace_column(self.editor().active_cursor().line)\n315:     }\n316: \n317:     /// Get last non-whitespace column on current line\n318:     pub fn last_non_whitespace_column(&self) -> usize {\n319:         self.document()\n320:             .last_non_whitespace_column(self.editor().active_cursor().line)\n321:     }\n322: }",
